# Source-discovery selection decisions

This is the normalized content in its original document order with a source-discovery semantic overlay. The labels are annotations; the content beneath them remains the normalized source structure.

- **Green — SELECTED:** relevant, current material eligible for extraction.
- **Orange — SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY:** possibly relevant, time-bounded, or temporally unknown material that remains eligible for extraction.
- **Gray — HISTORICAL:** retained for audit but excluded from current terms.
- **Blue — FUTURE:** retained for audit but excluded from current terms.
- **White — NOT SELECTED / UNASSESSED:** unchanged content without an accepted current extraction decision.

---

},  
{  
    title: &quot;DOMMI&quot;,    
    company: &quot;Baghramyan Vardan Hakobi IE&quot;,

    category: &quot;furniture&quot;    
},  
        ]  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt; partner cards end  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
  
        let selectedCategoryElement = $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;]&quot;);  
        let filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);  
        let visibleCards = 20;  
   
        if(document.body.clientWidth &lt;= 576) {  
            visibleCards = 6;  
        }  
  
        function renderCards(cardsArr) {  
            const container = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-card-container&quot;);  
            container.innerHTML = &#x27;&#x27;;  
            const showMoreBtn = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-companies-show-more&quot;);  
          
            const cardsToShow = cardsArr.slice(0, visibleCards);  
              
            cardsToShow.forEach(card =&gt; {  
                const cardElement = document.createElement(&quot;div&quot;);  
                cardElement.className = &quot;partner-card__item&quot;;  
                cardElement.innerHTML = `  
                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-icon&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;img src=&quot;${card.img}&quot; alt=&quot;${card.title}&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;/div&gt;  
                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-info&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-small font-bold&quot;&gt;${card.title}&lt;/p&gt;  
                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-xsmall&quot;&gt;${card.company}&lt;/p&gt;  
                    &lt;/div&gt;  
                `;  
                container.appendChild(cardElement);  
            });  
  
            if (visibleCards &gt;= cardsArr.length) {  
                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;none&quot;;  
            } else {  
                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;flex&quot;;  
            }  
        }  
  
        function calculateCategory() {  
            let all = partnerCards.length;  
            let furniture = 0;  
            let appliances = 0;  
            let automobileIndustry = 0;  
            let medicalServices = 0;  
            let courses = 0;  
            let solarSystems = 0;  
            let other = 0;  
            let touristServices = 0;  
            let sport = 0;  
            let constructionSupplies = 0;  
  
            partnerCards.map((item) =&gt; {  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;furniture&quot;)) {  
                    furniture++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;appliances&quot;)) {  
                    appliances++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;automobile-industry&quot;)) {  
                    automobileIndustry++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;medical-services&quot;)) {  
                    medicalServices++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;courses&quot;)) {  
                    courses++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;solar-systems&quot;)) {  
                    solarSystems++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;other&quot;)) {  
                    other++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;tourist-services&quot;)) {  
                    touristServices++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;sport&quot;)) {  
                    sport++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;construction-supplies&quot;)) {  
                    constructionSupplies++;  
                }  
            });  
              
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(all);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;furniture&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(furniture);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;appliances&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(appliances);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;automobile-industry&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(automobileIndustry);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;medical-services&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(medicalServices);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;courses&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(courses);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;solar-systems&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(solarSystems);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;other&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(other);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;tourist-services&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(touristServices);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;sport&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(sport);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;construction-supplies&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(constructionSupplies);  
        }  
  
        function filterByCategory(element) {  
            $(&quot;.pills-tab__item&quot;).removeClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
            element.addClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
            const selectedCategory = element.data(&quot;category&quot;);  
  
            if(selectedCategory === &quot;all&quot;) {  
                filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);  
                renderCards(filteredCards);  
            } else {  
                filteredCards = partnerCards.filter((item) =&gt; item.category.includes(selectedCategory));  
                renderCards(filteredCards);  
            }  
  
            searchPrivileges($(&quot;.privileges__search-input&quot;).val().trim());  
  
            let mobileSelectIcon = element.find(&quot;svg&quot;).clone();  
            let mobileSelectText = element.find(&quot;p&quot;).text();  
  
            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown__text svg&quot;).html(mobileSelectIcon);  
            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown__text span&quot;).text(mobileSelectText);  
        }  
  
        function searchPrivileges(searchInput) {  
            let findItemCount = 0;  
  
            const armenianToEnglishMap = {  
                &#x27;ա&#x27;: &#x27;a&#x27;, &#x27;բ&#x27;: &#x27;b&#x27;, &#x27;գ&#x27;: &#x27;g&#x27;, &#x27;դ&#x27;: &#x27;d&#x27;, &#x27;ե&#x27;: &#x27;e&#x27;, &#x27;զ&#x27;: &#x27;z&#x27;, &#x27;է&#x27;: &#x27;e&#x27;, &#x27;ը&#x27;: &#x27;y&#x27;,  
                &#x27;թ&#x27;: &#x27;t&#x27;, &#x27;ժ&#x27;: &#x27;zh&#x27;, &#x27;ի&#x27;: &#x27;i&#x27;, &#x27;լ&#x27;: &#x27;l&#x27;, &#x27;խ&#x27;: &#x27;x&#x27;, &#x27;ծ&#x27;: &#x27;ts&#x27;, &#x27;կ&#x27;: &#x27;k&#x27;, &#x27;հ&#x27;: &#x27;h&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ձ&#x27;: &#x27;dz&#x27;, &#x27;ղ&#x27;: &#x27;gh&#x27;, &#x27;ճ&#x27;: &#x27;ch&#x27;, &#x27;մ&#x27;: &#x27;m&#x27;, &#x27;յ&#x27;: &#x27;y&#x27;, &#x27;ն&#x27;: &#x27;n&#x27;, &#x27;շ&#x27;: &#x27;sh&#x27;, &#x27;ո&#x27;: &#x27;o&#x27;,  
                &#x27;չ&#x27;: &#x27;ch&#x27;, &#x27;պ&#x27;: &#x27;p&#x27;, &#x27;ջ&#x27;: &#x27;j&#x27;, &#x27;ռ&#x27;: &#x27;r&#x27;, &#x27;ս&#x27;: &#x27;s&#x27;, &#x27;վ&#x27;: &#x27;v&#x27;, &#x27;տ&#x27;: &#x27;t&#x27;, &#x27;ր&#x27;: &#x27;r&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ց&#x27;: &#x27;ts&#x27;, &#x27;ու&#x27;: &#x27;u&#x27;, &#x27;փ&#x27;: &#x27;p&#x27;, &#x27;ք&#x27;: &#x27;q&#x27;, &#x27;և&#x27;: &#x27;ev&#x27;, &#x27;օ&#x27;: &#x27;o&#x27;, &#x27;ֆ&#x27;: &#x27;f&#x27;  
            };  
            const armenianToRussianMap = {  
                &#x27;ա&#x27;: &#x27;а&#x27;, &#x27;բ&#x27;: &#x27;б&#x27;, &#x27;գ&#x27;: &#x27;г&#x27;, &#x27;դ&#x27;: &#x27;д&#x27;, &#x27;ե&#x27;: &#x27;е&#x27;, &#x27;զ&#x27;: &#x27;з&#x27;, &#x27;է&#x27;: &#x27;э&#x27;, &#x27;ը&#x27;: &#x27;ы&#x27;,  
                &#x27;թ&#x27;: &#x27;т&#x27;, &#x27;ժ&#x27;: &#x27;ж&#x27;, &#x27;ի&#x27;: &#x27;и&#x27;, &#x27;լ&#x27;: &#x27;л&#x27;, &#x27;խ&#x27;: &#x27;х&#x27;, &#x27;ծ&#x27;: &#x27;ц&#x27;, &#x27;կ&#x27;: &#x27;к&#x27;, &#x27;հ&#x27;: &#x27;х&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ձ&#x27;: &#x27;дз&#x27;, &#x27;ղ&#x27;: &#x27;г&#x27;, &#x27;ճ&#x27;: &#x27;ч&#x27;, &#x27;մ&#x27;: &#x27;м&#x27;, &#x27;յ&#x27;: &#x27;й&#x27;, &#x27;ն&#x27;: &#x27;н&#x27;, &#x27;շ&#x27;: &#x27;ш&#x27;, &#x27;ո&#x27;: &#x27;о&#x27;,  
                &#x27;չ&#x27;: &#x27;ч&#x27;, &#x27;պ&#x27;: &#x27;п&#x27;, &#x27;ջ&#x27;: &#x27;дж&#x27;, &#x27;ռ&#x27;: &#x27;р&#x27;, &#x27;ս&#x27;: &#x27;с&#x27;, &#x27;վ&#x27;: &#x27;в&#x27;, &#x27;տ&#x27;: &#x27;т&#x27;, &#x27;ր&#x27;: &#x27;р&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ց&#x27;: &#x27;ц&#x27;, &#x27;ու&#x27;: &#x27;у&#x27;, &#x27;փ&#x27;: &#x27;п&#x27;, &#x27;ք&#x27;: &#x27;к&#x27;, &#x27;և&#x27;: &#x27;ев&#x27;, &#x27;օ&#x27;: &#x27;о&#x27;, &#x27;ֆ&#x27;: &#x27;ф&#x27;  
            };  
  
            function armenianToEnglish(text) {  
                return text.split(&#x27;&#x27;).map(char =&gt; armenianToEnglishMap[char] || char).join(&#x27;&#x27;);  
            }  
            function armenianToRussian(text) {  
                return text.split(&#x27;&#x27;).map(char =&gt; armenianToRussianMap[char] || char).join(&#x27;&#x27;);  
            }  
  
            const search = searchInput.toLowerCase();  
  
            let searchResult = filteredCards.filter((item) =&gt; item.title.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(search) || armenianToEnglish(item.title.toLocaleLowerCase()).includes(search) || armenianToRussian(item.title.toLocaleLowerCase()).includes(search));  
              
            renderCards(searchResult);  
  
            findItemCount = searchResult.length;  
            if(!findItemCount) {  
                $(&quot;.privileges-card__noresult&quot;).show();  
            } else {  
                $(&quot;.privileges-card__noresult&quot;).hide();  
            }  
        }  
  
        function showMoreCards() {  
            if(document.body.clientWidth &lt;= 576) {  
                visibleCards += 6;  
            } else {  
                visibleCards += 20;  
            }  
  
            filterByCategory(selectedCategoryElement);  
        }  
  
        renderCards(partnerCards);  
        calculateCategory();  
  
        $(&quot;.pills-tab__item&quot;).on(&quot;click&quot;, function() {  
            selectedCategoryElement = $(this);  
            filterByCategory($(this));  
        });  
  
        $(&quot;.privileges__search-btn&quot;).click(function() {  
            searchPrivileges($(&quot;.privileges__search-input&quot;).val().trim());  
        });  
  
        $(&#x27;.privileges__search-input&#x27;).keypress(function(e) {  
            if (e.which == 13) {  
                searchPrivileges($(this).val().trim());  
            }  
        });  
  
        $(&quot;#partner-companies-show-more&quot;).on(&quot;click&quot;, function() {  
            showMoreCards();  
        });  
  
        // mobile filter select  
        if($(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).length) {  
            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).click(function () {  
                $(&quot;#topbar&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                $(&quot;#chatbot-chat&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                $(&quot;#goToTop&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                $(&quot;#section9&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
  
                $(this).toggleClass(&quot;custom-dropdown__is-active&quot;);  
                $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
            });  
  
            $(document).mouseup(function (e) {  
                if ($(e.target).closest(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).length === 0) {  
                    $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).removeClass(&quot;custom-dropdown__is-active&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer&quot;).removeClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#topbar&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#chatbot-chat&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#goToTop&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#section9&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                }  
            });  
        }  
      
    }  
  
    $(document).ready(function() {  
        setTimeout(function () {  
            initPartnerCompanies();  
        }, 0);  
    });  
&lt;/script&gt;

    category: &quot;solar-systems&quot;    
},  
{  
    title: &quot;DOMMI&quot;,    
    company: &quot;Baghramyan Vardan Hakobi IE&quot;,

    category: &quot;furniture&quot;    
},  
        ]  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt; partner cards end  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;  
  
        let selectedCategoryElement = $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;]&quot;);  
        let filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);  
        let visibleCards = 20;  
   
        if(document.body.clientWidth &lt;= 576) {  
            visibleCards = 6;  
        }  
  
        function renderCards(cardsArr) {  
            const container = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-card-container&quot;);  
            container.innerHTML = &#x27;&#x27;;  
            const showMoreBtn = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-companies-show-more&quot;);  
          
            const cardsToShow = cardsArr.slice(0, visibleCards);  
              
            cardsToShow.forEach(card =&gt; {  
                const cardElement = document.createElement(&quot;div&quot;);  
                cardElement.className = &quot;partner-card__item&quot;;  
                cardElement.innerHTML = `  
                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-icon&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;img src=&quot;${card.img}&quot; alt=&quot;${card.title}&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;/div&gt;  
                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-info&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-small font-bold&quot;&gt;${card.title}&lt;/p&gt;  
                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-xsmall&quot;&gt;${card.company}&lt;/p&gt;  
                    &lt;/div&gt;  
                `;  
                container.appendChild(cardElement);  
            });  
  
            if (visibleCards &gt;= cardsArr.length) {  
                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;none&quot;;  
            } else {  
                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;flex&quot;;  
            }  
        }  
  
        function calculateCategory() {  
            let all = partnerCards.length;  
            let furniture = 0;  
            let appliances = 0;  
            let automobileIndustry = 0;  
            let medicalServices = 0;  
            let courses = 0;  
            let solarSystems = 0;  
            let other = 0;  
            let touristServices = 0;  
            let sport = 0;  
            let constructionSupplies = 0;  
  
            partnerCards.map((item) =&gt; {  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;furniture&quot;)) {  
                    furniture++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;appliances&quot;)) {  
                    appliances++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;automobile-industry&quot;)) {  
                    automobileIndustry++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;medical-services&quot;)) {  
                    medicalServices++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;courses&quot;)) {  
                    courses++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;solar-systems&quot;)) {  
                    solarSystems++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;other&quot;)) {  
                    other++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;tourist-services&quot;)) {  
                    touristServices++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;sport&quot;)) {  
                    sport++;  
                }  
                if(item.category.includes(&quot;construction-supplies&quot;)) {  
                    constructionSupplies++;  
                }  
            });  
              
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(all);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;furniture&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(furniture);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;appliances&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(appliances);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;automobile-industry&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(automobileIndustry);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;medical-services&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(medicalServices);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;courses&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(courses);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;solar-systems&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(solarSystems);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;other&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(other);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;tourist-services&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(touristServices);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;sport&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(sport);  
            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;construction-supplies&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(constructionSupplies);  
        }  
  
        function filterByCategory(element) {  
            $(&quot;.pills-tab__item&quot;).removeClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
            element.addClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
            const selectedCategory = element.data(&quot;category&quot;);  
  
            if(selectedCategory === &quot;all&quot;) {  
                filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);  
                renderCards(filteredCards);  
            } else {  
                filteredCards = partnerCards.filter((item) =&gt; item.category.includes(selectedCategory));  
                renderCards(filteredCards);  
            }  
  
            searchPrivileges($(&quot;.privileges__search-input&quot;).val().trim());  
  
            let mobileSelectIcon = element.find(&quot;svg&quot;).clone();  
            let mobileSelectText = element.find(&quot;p&quot;).text();  
  
            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown__text svg&quot;).html(mobileSelectIcon);  
            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown__text span&quot;).text(mobileSelectText);  
        }  
  
        function searchPrivileges(searchInput) {  
            let findItemCount = 0;  
  
            const armenianToEnglishMap = {  
                &#x27;ա&#x27;: &#x27;a&#x27;, &#x27;բ&#x27;: &#x27;b&#x27;, &#x27;գ&#x27;: &#x27;g&#x27;, &#x27;դ&#x27;: &#x27;d&#x27;, &#x27;ե&#x27;: &#x27;e&#x27;, &#x27;զ&#x27;: &#x27;z&#x27;, &#x27;է&#x27;: &#x27;e&#x27;, &#x27;ը&#x27;: &#x27;y&#x27;,  
                &#x27;թ&#x27;: &#x27;t&#x27;, &#x27;ժ&#x27;: &#x27;zh&#x27;, &#x27;ի&#x27;: &#x27;i&#x27;, &#x27;լ&#x27;: &#x27;l&#x27;, &#x27;խ&#x27;: &#x27;x&#x27;, &#x27;ծ&#x27;: &#x27;ts&#x27;, &#x27;կ&#x27;: &#x27;k&#x27;, &#x27;հ&#x27;: &#x27;h&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ձ&#x27;: &#x27;dz&#x27;, &#x27;ղ&#x27;: &#x27;gh&#x27;, &#x27;ճ&#x27;: &#x27;ch&#x27;, &#x27;մ&#x27;: &#x27;m&#x27;, &#x27;յ&#x27;: &#x27;y&#x27;, &#x27;ն&#x27;: &#x27;n&#x27;, &#x27;շ&#x27;: &#x27;sh&#x27;, &#x27;ո&#x27;: &#x27;o&#x27;,  
                &#x27;չ&#x27;: &#x27;ch&#x27;, &#x27;պ&#x27;: &#x27;p&#x27;, &#x27;ջ&#x27;: &#x27;j&#x27;, &#x27;ռ&#x27;: &#x27;r&#x27;, &#x27;ս&#x27;: &#x27;s&#x27;, &#x27;վ&#x27;: &#x27;v&#x27;, &#x27;տ&#x27;: &#x27;t&#x27;, &#x27;ր&#x27;: &#x27;r&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ց&#x27;: &#x27;ts&#x27;, &#x27;ու&#x27;: &#x27;u&#x27;, &#x27;փ&#x27;: &#x27;p&#x27;, &#x27;ք&#x27;: &#x27;q&#x27;, &#x27;և&#x27;: &#x27;ev&#x27;, &#x27;օ&#x27;: &#x27;o&#x27;, &#x27;ֆ&#x27;: &#x27;f&#x27;  
            };  
            const armenianToRussianMap = {  
                &#x27;ա&#x27;: &#x27;а&#x27;, &#x27;բ&#x27;: &#x27;б&#x27;, &#x27;գ&#x27;: &#x27;г&#x27;, &#x27;դ&#x27;: &#x27;д&#x27;, &#x27;ե&#x27;: &#x27;е&#x27;, &#x27;զ&#x27;: &#x27;з&#x27;, &#x27;է&#x27;: &#x27;э&#x27;, &#x27;ը&#x27;: &#x27;ы&#x27;,  
                &#x27;թ&#x27;: &#x27;т&#x27;, &#x27;ժ&#x27;: &#x27;ж&#x27;, &#x27;ի&#x27;: &#x27;и&#x27;, &#x27;լ&#x27;: &#x27;л&#x27;, &#x27;խ&#x27;: &#x27;х&#x27;, &#x27;ծ&#x27;: &#x27;ц&#x27;, &#x27;կ&#x27;: &#x27;к&#x27;, &#x27;հ&#x27;: &#x27;х&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ձ&#x27;: &#x27;дз&#x27;, &#x27;ղ&#x27;: &#x27;г&#x27;, &#x27;ճ&#x27;: &#x27;ч&#x27;, &#x27;մ&#x27;: &#x27;м&#x27;, &#x27;յ&#x27;: &#x27;й&#x27;, &#x27;ն&#x27;: &#x27;н&#x27;, &#x27;շ&#x27;: &#x27;ш&#x27;, &#x27;ո&#x27;: &#x27;о&#x27;,  
                &#x27;չ&#x27;: &#x27;ч&#x27;, &#x27;պ&#x27;: &#x27;п&#x27;, &#x27;ջ&#x27;: &#x27;дж&#x27;, &#x27;ռ&#x27;: &#x27;р&#x27;, &#x27;ս&#x27;: &#x27;с&#x27;, &#x27;վ&#x27;: &#x27;в&#x27;, &#x27;տ&#x27;: &#x27;т&#x27;, &#x27;ր&#x27;: &#x27;р&#x27;,  
                &#x27;ց&#x27;: &#x27;ц&#x27;, &#x27;ու&#x27;: &#x27;у&#x27;, &#x27;փ&#x27;: &#x27;п&#x27;, &#x27;ք&#x27;: &#x27;к&#x27;, &#x27;և&#x27;: &#x27;ев&#x27;, &#x27;օ&#x27;: &#x27;о&#x27;, &#x27;ֆ&#x27;: &#x27;ф&#x27;  
            };  
  
            function armenianToEnglish(text) {  
                return text.split(&#x27;&#x27;).map(char =&gt; armenianToEnglishMap[char] || char).join(&#x27;&#x27;);  
            }  
            function armenianToRussian(text) {  
                return text.split(&#x27;&#x27;).map(char =&gt; armenianToRussianMap[char] || char).join(&#x27;&#x27;);  
            }  
  
            const search = searchInput.toLowerCase();  
  
            let searchResult = filteredCards.filter((item) =&gt; item.title.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(search) || armenianToEnglish(item.title.toLocaleLowerCase()).includes(search) || armenianToRussian(item.title.toLocaleLowerCase()).includes(search));  
              
            renderCards(searchResult);  
  
            findItemCount = searchResult.length;  
            if(!findItemCount) {  
                $(&quot;.privileges-card__noresult&quot;).show();  
            } else {  
                $(&quot;.privileges-card__noresult&quot;).hide();  
            }  
        }  
  
        function showMoreCards() {  
            if(document.body.clientWidth &lt;= 576) {  
                visibleCards += 6;  
            } else {  
                visibleCards += 20;  
            }  
  
            filterByCategory(selectedCategoryElement);  
        }  
  
        renderCards(partnerCards);  
        calculateCategory();  
  
        $(&quot;.pills-tab__item&quot;).on(&quot;click&quot;, function() {  
            selectedCategoryElement = $(this);  
            filterByCategory($(this));  
        });  
  
        $(&quot;.privileges__search-btn&quot;).click(function() {  
            searchPrivileges($(&quot;.privileges__search-input&quot;).val().trim());  
        });  
  
        $(&#x27;.privileges__search-input&#x27;).keypress(function(e) {  
            if (e.which == 13) {  
                searchPrivileges($(this).val().trim());  
            }  
        });  
  
        $(&quot;#partner-companies-show-more&quot;).on(&quot;click&quot;, function() {  
            showMoreCards();  
        });  
  
        // mobile filter select  
        if($(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).length) {  
            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).click(function () {  
                $(&quot;#topbar&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                $(&quot;#chatbot-chat&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                $(&quot;#goToTop&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                $(&quot;#section9&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
  
                $(this).toggleClass(&quot;custom-dropdown__is-active&quot;);  
                $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
            });  
  
            $(document).mouseup(function (e) {  
                if ($(e.target).closest(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).length === 0) {  
                    $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).removeClass(&quot;custom-dropdown__is-active&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer&quot;).removeClass(&quot;active&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#topbar&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#chatbot-chat&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#goToTop&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                    $(&quot;#section9&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);  
                }  
            });  
        }  
      
    }  
  
    $(document).ready(function() {  
        setTimeout(function () {  
            initPartnerCompanies();  
        }, 0);  
    });  
&lt;/script&gt;
